Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on modeling structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 375. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 108 with relations in degree 3.

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to modeling structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 375/108 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.

Theorem 213.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Given a modeling algebra A over field F with structural decomposition conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 164. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 244/3.

Proof: By the structural decomposition principle applied to modeling theory, we decompose the problem into 164 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 244. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(3/n). QED.
